entry:

  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()

  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t441
}

define internal ptr @will.task.error.clone(ptr %data) {
entry:
  %t449 = getelementptr %willrt.task_error, ptr null, i32 1
  %t450 = ptrtoint ptr %t449 to i64
  %t451 = call ptr @willrt_alloc(i64 %t450)
  call void @memcpy(ptr %t451, ptr %data, i64 %t450)
  %t452 = getelementptr inbounds %willrt.task_error, ptr %data, i32 0, i32 0, i32 0
  %t453 = load ptr, ptr %t452
  %t454 = getelementptr inbounds %willrt.task_error, ptr %data, i32 0, i32 0, i32 1
  %t455 = load i64, ptr %t454
  %t456 = icmp ne ptr %t453, null
  %t457 = icmp ugt i64 %t455, 0
  %t458 = and i1 %t456, %t457
  br i1 %t458, label %task.error.copy.41, label %task.error.empty.41
task.error.copy.41:
  %t459 = call ptr @willrt_alloc(i64 %t455)
  call void @memcpy(ptr %t459, ptr %t453, i64 %t455)
  %t460 = getelementptr inbounds %willrt.task_error, ptr %t451, i32 0, i32 0, i32 0
  store ptr %t459, ptr %t460
  br label %task.error.msg.done.41
task.error.empty.41:
  %t461 = getelementptr inbounds %willrt.task_error, ptr %t451, i32 0, i32 0, i32 0
  store ptr null, ptr %t461
  br label %task.error.msg.done.41
task.error.msg.done.41:
  ret ptr %t451
}
